                                            131 +                                                                                                                 132 +- **Model and work are unchanged.** It uses the same float32 weights, architecture, RoPE, KV cache (layer, seq_l     +en, kv_dim) and greedy argmax with the lowest index winning ties. Logits are computed at every forwarded positio     +n, including forced prompt positions in GENERATE.                                                                133 +- **Known token sequences are forwarded in chunks** of up to 512 positions (prompts, FIXED, LOGITS), using block      +ed `sgemm`. Single tokens use blocked `sgemv`.                                                                   134 +- **Element-wise work** uses vDSP/vForce or plain scalar C:                                                      135 +  - rmsnorm: `vDSP_svesq`                                                                                        136 +  - softmax: `vDSP_maxv`, `vvexpf`, `vDSP_sve`, `vDSP_vsdiv`                                                     137 +  - SwiGLU: vDSP/vForce, as described in change 2                                                                138 +  - RoPE: scalar C, with a cos/sin table built from the reference's exact float expressions                       139 +                                                                                                                 140 +  There are no hand-written matrix loops and no CPU intrinsics.                                                  141 +- **Single thread.** No threads are created and no thread counts are set.                                        142 +                                                                                                                 143 +## Interface choices where INTERFACE.md is silent (unchanged from v1)                                             144 +                                                                                                                 145 +- **GENERATE stop order:** if the chosen token is 1, it ends with `stop`. Otherwise the token is emitted; then i     +f count == max_new it ends with `max`. If the next forward position would be seq_len, it ends with `context`.    146 +- **Prompt length:** a prompt longer than seq_len gives `DONE 0 context`. A prompt of exactly seq_len emits one      +token, as the reference does.                                                                                     147 +- **`GENERATE 0`** answers `DONE 0 max` without running the model.                                               148 +- **PIECE** prints `HEX ` followed by the bytes of the piece as a C string. An empty piece (for example `PIECE 1     + 29871`, or `<0x00>`) gives `HEX ` with nothing after it.                                                        149 +- **Malformed commands,** out-of-range ids, and FIXED/LOGITS with more than seq_len ids get an `ERR …` line.     150 +- **KV cache between commands:** it is not re-zeroed. Every command writes positions 0..n-1 before reading them       +and never reads past the current position.                                                                       151 +                                                                                                                 152 +## v1 history (round 1)                                                                                          153 +                                                                                                                  154 +v1 kept the row-major layout with fused QKV and W1/W3 weights, and used `sgemv`/`sgemm` NoTrans. It passed the s     +ame checks and ran at ≈79 tok/s decode.                                                                         ╌╌╌╌╌╌╌╌╌╌╌╌╌╌╌╌╌╌╌╌╌╌╌╌╌╌╌╌╌╌╌╌╌╌╌╌╌╌╌╌╌╌╌╌╌╌╌╌╌╌╌╌╌╌╌╌╌╌╌╌╌╌╌╌╌╌╌╌╌╌╌╌╌╌╌╌╌╌╌╌╌╌╌╌╌╌╌╌╌╌╌╌╌╌╌╌╌╌╌╌╌╌╌╌╌╌╌╌╌╌╌╌╌╌╌╌╌╌╌╌ Do you want to overwrite NOTES.md?❯1. 79.5. The rebuilt binary passesboth check.py runs (default and 3 prompts × 64 tokens). The largest logit error is 2.6e-5, against a tolerance of5e-4.What profiling showed-Decode is entirely limited by reading the weights. 97% of the time went to the weight matrix-vector products,whichread memory at only 33–37 GB/s. Plain memory reads on this M1 reach 63 GB/s. My v1 notes said 34 GB/s was thehardware limit; that was wrong, and NOTES.md now says so.-Weight layout was the cause. Stored transposed, in contiguous blocks 256–384 columns wide, sgemv reads the weightsat about 63 GB/s.-Smaller costs: SwiGLU was 9% of prompt processing (one scalar expf per element). Long-context attention cost about4ms per token.-Tokenizer: the reference's merge loop took 1.6 s on a 900-word prompt.Changes1.Weights are stored transposed in 256-column blocks, with one Accelerate call per block. Prompt processing losesnothing from this layout.2.Q/K/V and W1/W3 are now separate matrices. K and V are written directly into the KV cache, and SwiGLU runs asafewwhole-buffer vDSP/vForce calls in the reference's order of operations.3.The embedding table is read from the transposed classifier, so the weights are only held in memory once.4.Loading transposes straight from an mmap of the checkpoint, with no staging copy.5.Attention over a chunk of prompt tokens skips the masked-out region. Everything the reference computes is stillcomputed.6.From 160 positions onwards, single-token attention handles all heads with two sgemm calls per layer.Thisadds    multiplications by zerobutremovesnoneofthereference'swork.Below160theoldper-headpathiskeptbecause     it is faster there.7.The tokenizer caches the merge candidate for each adjacent pair.Itsoutputisidenticaltothereferencealgorithm    on 514 test texts, including the fixtures and randomUnicode.4v1kepttherow-majorlayoutwithfusedQKVandW1/W3weights,andused`sgemv`/`sgemm`NoTrans.Itpa   ssed the same checks and ranat ≈79 tok/s decoe.                                              Ran 1 shell command ⏺Generationonthesuppliedworkloadsisnowabout1.6×faster:121–132tok/s,upfrom79.5.Therebuiltbinarypasses both check.pyruns (default and 3 prompts ×64 tokens).
